Motivation: 现有的RAG系统对抗研究主要集中于文本或内存操纵，而对遥感多模态RAG中视觉输入阶段的检索安全威胁尚缺乏探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究输入空间中大气扰动对证据检索的影响。

Method: CloudWeb在遥感图像上叠加可参数化的云层和雾霾样图案，通过面向检索的目标优化扰动：将对抗性图像嵌入拉向目标大气证据、抑制源场景证据、强制排名分离，并正则化自然性和覆盖范围。

Result: 在七个数据集的遥感RAG基准上评估了五种CLIP风格的检索器（如GeoRSCLIP、RemoteCLIP等），结果显示CloudWeb显著提升了天气相关证据在前五名结果中的比例（例如GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32上从0.71%升至43.29%），且下游生成出现明显天气幻觉和语义偏移。

Conclusion: 自然外观的大气变化可在生成前就破坏证据检索，揭示了遥感多模态RAG系统在检索阶段的一个实际失效模式，强调了输入安全性的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal RAG systems increasingly rely on vision-language retrievers to ground visual queries in external textual evidence. Existing adversarial studies on RAG mainly manipulate the retrieval corpus or memory, while attacks on vision-language and remote sensing models typically target end-task predictions. Input-space threats to the evidence retrieval stage of remote sensing multimodal RAG remain underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CloudWeb, an atmospheric retrieval hijacking attack that modifies only the input image while keeping the retriever, generator, and knowledge base fixed at deployment. CloudWeb overlays parameterized cloud- and haze-like patterns on remote sensing images and optimizes them with a retrieval-oriented objective that pulls adversarial image embeddings toward target atmospheric evidence, suppresses source-scene evidence, enforces rank separation, and regularizes naturalness and coverage. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study of retrieval-stage atmospheric evidence hijacking in remote sensing multimodal RAG. We evaluate CloudWeb on a seven-dataset remote sensing RAG benchmark with five CLIP-style retrievers, including GeoRSCLIP, RemoteCLIP, OpenAI CLIP, and OpenCLIP, together with downstream vision-language generators. Across retrievers, CloudWeb consistently outperforms clean retrieval, handcrafted atmospheric baselines, random cloud perturbations, and fixed variants in injecting weather-related evidence into top-ranked results. On GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32, Weather@5 increases from 0.71\% to 43.29\%. Downstream generation further shows measurable weather hallucination and semantic shift, indicating that retrieval-stage hijacking can propagate to the final RAG response. These findings reveal a practical failure mode: natural-looking atmospheric changes can compromise evidence retrieval before generation begins.

</details>


### [39] [SplatWeaver: Learning to Allocate Gaussian Primitives for Generalizable Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07287)
*Yecong Wan,Fan Li,Mingwen Shao,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWeaver是一种可泛化的新型视图合成框架，通过动态分配高斯基元数量，在保持高效的同时提升了渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对每个像素或体素使用固定数量的高斯基元，忽略了场景中空间复杂度的变化，导致平滑区域资源浪费、细节区域表达不足。

Method: 提出SplatWeaver，引入基数高斯专家和像素级路由机制，每个专家负责生成特定数量的基元（0到M），路由机制自适应决定各位置应分配的基元数量；结合高频先验与引导模块及路由正则化，利用高频结构线索指导基元分配。

Result: 在多种场景下实验表明，SplatWeaver在使用更少高斯基元的情况下， consistently 优于当前最先进方法，生成更保真的新视图渲染结果。

Conclusion: 通过复杂度感知的动态基元分配机制，SplatWeaver实现了更高效且紧凑的3D场景表示，显著提升了通用新视图合成的性能。

Abstract: Generalizable novel view synthesis aims to render unseen views from uncalibrated input images without requiring per-scene optimization. Recent feed-forward approaches based on 3D Gaussian Splatting have achieved promising efficiency and rendering quality. However, most of them assign a fixed number of Gaussians to each pixel or voxel, ignoring the spatially varying complexity of real-world scenes. Such uniform allocation often wastes Gaussian primitives in smooth regions while providing insufficient capacity for fine structures, complex geometry, and high-frequency details. This motivates us to predict region-dependent primitive cardinalities rather than impose a fixed primitive budget everywhere, enabling a more expressive yet compact 3D scene representation. Therefore, we propose SplatWeaver, a generalizable novel view synthesis framework that is able to dynamically allocate Gaussian primitives over different regions in a feed-forward manner. Specifically, SplatWeaver introduces cardinality Gaussian experts and a pixel-level routing scheme, wherein each expert specializes in producing a specific number of primitives from 0 to M, and the routing scheme coordinates these experts to adaptively determine how many Gaussian primitives should be allocated to each spatial location. Moreover, SplatWeaver incorporates a high-frequency prior with attendant guidance module and routing regularization to stabilize expert selection and promote complexity-aware allocation. By leveraging high-frequency structural cues, the routing process is encouraged to assign more Gaussian primitives to fine structures, complex geometry, and textured regions, while suppressing redundant primitives in smooth areas. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios show that SplatWeaver consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, delivering more faithful novel-view renderings with fewer Gaussian primitives.

</details>


### [40] [Sword: Style-Robust World Models as Simulators via Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping for VLA Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07288)
*Jiaxuan Gao,Yongjian Guo,Zhong Guan,Wen Huang,Wanlun Ma,Xi Xiao,Junwu Xiong,Sheng Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Sword的鲁棒世界模型框架，通过结构引导的风格增强和动态潜在自举方法，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作模型在LIBERO基准上的泛化能力、生成质量与控制成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在用作特定环境（如LIBERO）的生成模拟器时，存在泛化能力差和长时程误差累积的问题，对初始状态扰动敏感，易产生级联幻觉，限制了其作为可靠模拟器的应用。

Method: 提出Sword框架，包含两项关键技术：1）结构引导的风格增强（Structure-Guided Style Augmentation），解耦交互环境的视觉纹理与任务相关动力学；2）动态潜在自举（Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping），在保持低内存消耗的同时维持训练与推理的一致性。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法在泛化性、生成质量、鲁棒性、状态预测保真度以及强化学习后训练的成功率方面均显著优于基线方法WoVR。

Conclusion: Sword框架有效缓解了世界模型在闭环 rollout 中的误差累积与感知扰动敏感问题，增强了其作为VLA模型想象空间的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with World Models has gained increasing attention. One representative approach treats learned World Models as generative simulators, enabling policy optimization entirely within "imagination." However, when deployed as simulators for specific environments such as the LIBERO benchmark, existing World Models often suffer from poor generalization and long-horizon error accumulation. During closed-loop rollouts, these models are highly sensitive to initial-state perturbations; minor changes in color, illumination, and other visual factors can trigger cascading hallucinations, leading to severe blurriness or overexposure. Moreover, long-horizon error accumulation further degrades the quality and fidelity of predicted future states. These issues limit the reliability of World Models as simulators. To mitigate these problems, we propose Sword, a robust World Model framework. Our method introduces Structure-Guided Style Augmentation to disentangle the visual textures of interactive environments from task-relevant dynamics, thereby improving generalization. We further propose Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping, which maintains consistency between training and inference while keeping memory consumption low. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method significantly outperforms the baseline WoVR in terms of generalization, generation quality, robustness, fidelity, and the success rate of reinforcement-learning post-training for VLA models.

</details>


### [41] [EgoPro-Bench: Benchmarking Personalized Proactive Interaction in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07299)
*Dongchuan Ran,Linyu Ou,Xueheng Li,Wenwen Tong,Chenxu Guo,Hewei Guo,Kaibing Wang,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoPro-Bench，一个基于第一人称视频流的新型基准，用于训练和评估多模态大模型的主动交互能力，通过模拟用户画像和精细化交互时序评估，显著提升了模型对用户意图理解和交互时机把握的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM主要为被动响应，缺乏持续环境感知和主动辅助能力；现有基准局限于警报场景，忽视个性化上下文和交互时机的精确评估。

Method: 构建包含12,000+训练视频和2,400测试视频的EgoPro-Bench，利用模拟用户画像生成多样化用户意图，并在12个领域中构建高保真人机交互数据；提出专门的评估协议与指标，训练支持高效推理和低延迟交互的主动模型，并引入“短思考，更好交互”原则，限制意图识别前的token预算以提升性能。

Result: 实验表明，EgoPro-Bench显著增强了MLLM对用户意图的理解能力，并能准确识别合适的人机交互时机。

Conclusion: EgoPro-Bench为下一代以用户为中心的主动交互智能体奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain primarily reactive, failing to continuously perceive environments or proactively assist users. While emerging benchmarks address proactivity, they are largely confined to alert scenarios, neglect personalized context, and fail to evaluate the precise timing of human-machine interactions (HMI).In this paper, we introduce EgoPro-Bench, a novel benchmark for training and evaluating proactive interaction capabilities based on streaming egocentric videos; it comprises 2,400 videos in the evaluation set and over 12,000 videos in the training set.Unlike previous works, EgoPro-Bench leverages simulated user profiles to generate diverse user intentions and to construct high-fidelity HMI data across 12 distinct domains.Subsequently, we propose a specialized evaluation protocol and metrics, train proactive interaction models designed for efficient reasoning and low-latency interaction on streaming video data, and conduct comprehensive evaluations.Furthermore, we introduce an interaction principle termed "short thinking, better interaction", which allocates a limited token budget prior to intent recognition, thereby enhancing interaction performance.The experiments demonstrate that EgoPro-Bench substantially enhances the intention understanding capabilities of MLLMs and enables accurate identification of appropriate timings for HMI, thereby laying a solid foundation for next-generation user-centric proactive interactive agents.

</details>


### [42] [Amortized-Precision Quantization for Early-Exit Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07317)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Amortized-Precision Quantization (APQ) 和 Mutual Adaptive Quantization with Early Exiting (MAQEE)，以解决Vision Transformers在低精度早期退出推理中的不稳定性问题，实现了显著的计算效率提升并保持了高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在多种视觉任务中表现优异，但其在低精度条件下的早期退出机制仍不稳定，现有量化方法无法有效应对量化噪声对动态推理路径的影响。

Method: 提出APQ，一种考虑层间随机暴露于量化噪声的利用率感知量化方法，并在此基础上设计MAQEE框架，联合优化退出阈值和比特宽度，实现显式的风险控制。

Result: MAQEE在分类、检测和分割任务上显著优于基线方法，最多减少95%的BOPs，同时准确率提升高达20%。

Conclusion: MAQEE通过联合优化量化策略与早期退出机制，在保证模型精度的同时大幅提升了推理效率，为ViTs的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance across vision tasks, yet their deployment with low-precision early exiting remains fragile. Existing quantization methods assume static full-depth execution, making them unstable when exit decisions are perturbed by quantization noise, which can amplify errors along dynamic inference paths. In this paper, we introduce Amortized-Precision Quantization (APQ), a utilization-aware formulation that accounts for layer-wise stochastic exposure to quantization noise and reveals depth-precision trade-offs. Building on APQ, we propose Mutual Adaptive Quantization with Early Exiting (MAQEE), a bi-level framework that jointly optimizes exit thresholds and bit-widths under explicit risk control to improve inference stability. MAQEE establishes a superior Pareto frontier in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off, reducing BOPs by up to 95% while maintaining accuracy and outperforming strong baselines by up to 20\% across classification, detection, and segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [43] [GEM: Generating LiDAR World Model via Deformable Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07326)
*Yang Wu,Zhaojiang Liu,Qiang Meng,Youquan Liu,Renliang Weng,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LiDAR的生成式世界模型GEM，利用可变形Mamba架构提升自动驾驶中环境建模的保真度与想象能力，在多个基准上实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LiDAR点云的无序性和动态物体与静态结构难以区分，现有LiDAR世界模型发展滞后，本文旨在解决这两个核心挑战。

Method: 提出GEM模型：首先通过定制的LiDAR场景分词器将LiDAR扫描转换为紧凑表示；然后采用无监督方式分离动态与静态特征；引入三路径可变形Mamba进行选择性扫描和自适应门控融合；可选集成规划器和BEV布局控制器以支持自主推演和“假设”场景生成。

Result: 实验表明GEM在多种基准和评估设置下均达到最先进水平，显著提升了时空建模能力和生成质量。

Conclusion: GEM通过结合Mamba架构与动态-静态特征解耦策略，有效解决了LiDAR世界模型的关键难题，展现出强大的环境模拟与前瞻性推理能力。

Abstract: World models, which simulate environmental dynamics and generate sensor observations, are gaining increasing attention in autonomous driving. However, progress in LiDAR-based world models has lagged behind those built on camera videos or occupancy data, primarily due to two core challenges: the inherent disorder of LiDAR point clouds and the difficulty of distinguishing dynamic objects from static structures. To address these issues, we propose GEM: a Generative LiDAR world model that leverages deformable mamba architecture, significantly improving fidelity and imaginative capability. Specifically, leveraging the structural similarity between sequential laser scanning and Mamba's processing mechanism, we first tokenize LiDAR sweeps into compact representations via a custom LiDAR scene tokenizer. After unsupervised disentanglement of tokenized features via a dynamic-static separator, a tri-path deformable Mamba is introduced to perform selective scanning and adaptive gating fusion over the disentangled features, leading to enhanced spatial-temporal understanding of the world evolution. Optionally, a planner and a BEV layout controller can be integrated to explore the model's capability for autonomous rollout and its potential to generate ``what-if" scenarios. Extensive experiments show that GEM achieves state-of-the-art performances across diverse benchmarks and evaluation settings, demonstrating its superiority and effectiveness. Project page: https://github.com/wuyang98/GEM.

</details>


### [44] [GC-ART: Global Learnable Second-Order Rational Tone Curves for Illumination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07329)
*Wei Huang,Joyce Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GC-ART是一种轻量级可微分预处理模块，通过预测基于通道软直方图的有理色调曲线来增强图像分类鲁棒性，在对比度退化等情况下表现优于基线和其他学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升图像分类模型在各种光照退化情况下的鲁棒性，同时保持轻量化和端到端可训练性，设计一种有效的学习型色调映射预处理模块。

Method: GC-ART使用一个643参数的MLP从每通道软直方图预测端点固定的有理色调曲线，并逐点应用该曲线进行预处理；模块与分类器联合端到端训练，采用交叉熵损失和软单调性约束。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ResNet-18上，GC-ART保持了原始准确率，优于基线模型在乘性变暗和对比度退化上的表现（48.45% vs 基线46.27%），且FLOPs显著低于卷积类学习增强器。

Conclusion: 基于直方图条件的有理曲线能够学习有用的全局色调校正（如扩展对比度），并通过逐点映射保持边缘位置，GC-ART在效率和性能之间实现了良好平衡，具有进一步优化潜力。

Abstract: We introduce GC-ART (Global Curve Adaptive Rational Tone-mapping), a lightweight differentiable pre-processing module for robust image classification. GC-ART predicts an endpoint-pinned rational tone curve from per-channel soft histograms using a 643-parameter MLP, then applies the curve pointwise before the classifier. The module is trained end-to-end with cross-entropy and a soft monotonicity penalty. On CIFAR-10 with a CIFAR-style ResNet-18, GC-ART matches clean accuracy with the unenhanced baseline and other learned enhancers, improves over the baseline on multiplicative darkening, and achieves the best learned-method result on contrast corruption (48.45% vs. 46.27% for the baseline and 47.13% for Zero-DCE++). These results suggest that histogram-conditioned rational curves can learn useful global tone corrections, including contrast-expanding behavior, while preserving edge locations by construction through pointwise mapping. GC-ART also uses substantially fewer FLOPs than convolutional learned enhancers at 32 x 32. The current hyperparameters are untuned, leaving room for systematic improvement.

</details>


### [45] [Disambiguating 2D-3D Correspondences in Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields for Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07351)
*Miso Lee,Sangeek Hyun,Yerim Jeon,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SplitGS-Loc，一种专为定位设计的高斯点阵特征场构建框架，通过利用高斯属性解决光度优化GSFF在2D-3D匹配中的固有缺陷，实现无需每场景训练或迭代位姿优化的高精度高效定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度优化的高斯点阵特征场（GSFF）在视觉定位中存在2D-3D匹配不稳定的问题，主要源于高斯体素导致的多对一像素映射以及缺乏多视角一致性的冗余高斯，因此需要一种更适用于定位任务的特征场构建方法。

Method: 提出SplitGS-Loc框架，核心是基于混合高斯的分裂策略，将每个高斯分解为更小的高斯，实现从多对一到一对一的精确2D-3D对应；同时利用GS渲染中的组合权重筛选跨多视图具有一致贡献的高斯，并通过强像素-高斯关联聚合判别性特征，增强多视角一致性。

Result: 构建出紧凑且具判别性的特征场，使PnP位姿估计更稳定，收敛性更好，在多个定位基准上达到最先进性能。实验验证了SplitGS-Loc在不依赖每场景训练或迭代优化的情况下，显著提升了基于光度GSFF的定位精度与效率。

Conclusion: SplitGS-Loc通过挖掘高斯属性有效解决了传统光度优化GSFF在视觉定位中的根本问题，为高斯点阵应用于2D-3D匹配提供了新的设计思路，并拓展了其在高效精准定位中的应用潜力。

Abstract: While Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields (GSFFs) have shown promise for visual localization, this paper highlights that photometrically optimized GSFFs are inherently ill-suited for 2D-3D matching. The volumetric extent of each Gaussian induces many-to-one pixel-to-point mappings that destabilize PnP-based pose estimation, while photometric optimization gives rise to superfluous Gaussians devoid of multi-view consistency. To address these issues, we propose SplitGS-Loc, a localization-specialized GSFFs construction framework that disambiguates 2D-3D correspondences by exploiting Gaussian attributes. Our key design, Mixture-of-Gaussians-based splitting, decomposes each Gaussian into smaller Gaussians, replacing ambiguous many-to-one with precise one-to-one correspondences. In parallel, we exploit composition weights from GS rasterization to select Gaussians that significantly and consistently contribute across multiple views and aggregate discriminative features through strong pixel-Gaussian associations, enforcing multi-view consistency. The resulting compact yet discriminative feature fields enable stable PnP convergence, achieving state-of-the-art performance on localization benchmarks. Extensive experiments validate that SplitGS-Loc extends the utility of photometric GSFFs to accurate and efficient localization by exploiting Gaussian attributes, without per-scene training or iterative pose refinement.

</details>


### [46] [TTF: Temporal Token Fusion for Efficient Video-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07355)
*Simin Huo,Ning LI*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练、即插即用的视频语言模型前处理压缩方法Temporal Token Fusion（TTF），通过局部窗口相似性搜索融合冗余视觉令牌，在显著减少计算量的同时保持几乎全部准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型中随着视频长度增加，视觉令牌数量迅速增长，导致推理成本高昂，尤其是LLM预填充阶段成为吞吐瓶颈。现有方法依赖全局相似性或注意力引导压缩，存在增益偏差问题。

Method: 设计了一种训练免费的预LLM令牌压缩框架TTF，利用视频中的时间冗余结构：自动选择锚帧，并对后续每帧进行局部窗口（如3×3）相似性搜索，将超过阈值的令牌进行融合，同时通过坐标重对齐保持位置一致性。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上以0.70阈值实现了约67%的视觉令牌移除，保留了99.5%的基线精度，仅引入约0.16 GFLOPs匹配开销。

Conclusion: TTF为视频理解任务提供了一个高效、实用的视觉令牌压缩解决方案，可无缝集成到现有VLM流水线中。

Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) face rapid inference costs as visual token counts scale with video length. For example, 32 frames at $448{\times}448$ resolution already yield >8,000 visual tokens in Qwen3-VL, making LLM prefill the dominant throughput bottleneck. Existing methods often rely on global similarity or attention-guided compression, incurring offsets to their gains. We propose \textbf{Temporal Token Fusion (TTF)}, a training-free, plug-and-play pre-LLM token compression framework that exploits structured temporal redundancy in video. TTF automatically selects an anchor frame, then for each subsequent frame, performs a local window similarity search (e.g.,$3\times 3$), fusing tokens that exceed a threshold. The compressed sequence maintains positional consistency across both prefill and decoding through coordinate realignment, enabling seamless integration with existing VLM pipelines. On Qwen3-VL-8B with threshold t=0.70, TTF removes about 67\% of visual tokens while retaining 99.5\% of the baseline accuracy and introducing only ${\approx}0.16$\,GFLOPs of matching overhead. Overall, TTF offers a practical, efficient solution for video understanding. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}

</details>


### [47] [UniD-Shift: Towards Unified Semantic Segmentation via Interpretable Share-Private Multimodal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07356)
*Shuai Zhang,Zhecheng Shi,Zhuxiao Li,Jing Ou,Tengxi Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于2D-3D语义分割的统一多模态框架，通过分解共享与私有特征子空间，实现跨模态语义对齐与模态特异性保留，提升了大尺度3D点云分割的准确性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LiDAR稀疏采样和图像视角依赖的几何失真，现有方法在跨模态对齐和稳定融合方面存在困难；作者认为2D图像与3D点云的特征学习具有共同语义，但也包含模态特有信息，因此需要统一框架来协同利用两种模态的优势。

Method: 结合基于SAM的视觉编码器和基于SPTNet的几何编码器提取2D和3D特征，并将特征显式分解为共享子空间（共通语义）和私有子空间（模态特有），通过轻量级注意力融合模块聚合共享特征，并设计正则化训练目标以保证语义对齐和子空间独立性。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和nuScenes基准上均取得了优于代表性多模态基线方法的分割精度，计算效率具有竞争力；在nuScenes USA-Singapore的跨域实验中表现出稳定的性能，验证了模型的良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了2D与3D模态之间的语义共享与差异保留，增强了跨模态融合的稳定性与泛化性，为大规模3D场景理解提供了高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of large-scale 3D point clouds is crucial for applications such as autonomous driving and urban digital twins. However, the sparse sampling pattern of LiDAR and the view-dependent geometric distortion in image observations complicate cross-modal alignment and hinder stable fusion. Inspired by the fact that 2D images captured by cameras are representations of the 3D world, we recognize that the features learned from 2D and 3D segmentation share some common semantics, while other aspects remain modality-specific. This insight motivates a unified multimodal framework for joint 2D-3D semantic segmentation. We combine a SAM-based vision encoder with a SPTNet-based geometric encoder to extract complementary semantic and geometric representations. The resulting features from both modalities are explicitly decomposed into shared and private subspaces, where the shared components summarize semantic factors common to both domains, and the private components preserve properties that are unique to each modality. A lightweight attention-based fusion module aggregates the shared features into a consistent cross-modal representation, and a regularized training objective ensures both semantic alignment and subspace independence. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and nuScenes benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in segmentation accuracy over representative multimodal baselines, accompanied by competitive computational efficiency. Cross-domain evaluation on nuScenes USA-Singapore shows stable performance under distribution shifts, demonstrating strong generalization. The implementation code is publicly available at: https://github.com/shuaizhang69/UniD-Shift.

</details>


### [48] [RELO: Reinforcement Learning to Localize for Visual Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07379)
*Xin Chen,Chuanyu Sun,Jiao Xu,Houwen Peng,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu,Kede Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的目标定位方法RELO，用于视觉目标跟踪，通过奖励驱动的方式替代传统手工先验，提升了跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法使用手工设计的空间先验（如热图）进行目标定位，这些先验与跟踪优化目标（如IoU和AUC）不一致，导致性能受限。

Method: 将目标定位建模为马尔可夫决策过程，采用强化学习训练定位策略，奖励函数结合帧级别IoU和序列级别AUC；并引入层对齐的时间令牌传播以增强跨帧语义一致性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得优越结果，在LaSOText数据集上无需模板更新即达到57.5% AUC。

Conclusion: 奖励驱动的定位方法是先验驱动方法的有效替代方案，能更紧密地对齐优化目标，提升视觉目标跟踪性能。

Abstract: Conventional visual object trackers localize targets using handcrafted spatial priors, often in the form of heatmaps. Such priors provide only surrogate supervision and are poorly aligned with tracking optimization and evaluation metrics, such as intersection over union (IoU) and area under the success curve (AUC). Here, we introduce RELO, a REinforcement-learning-to-LOcalize method for visual object tracking that formulates target localization as a Markov decision process. Specifically, RELO replaces handcrafted spatial priors with a localization policy learned over spatial positions via reinforcement learning, with rewards combining frame-level IoU and sequence-level AUC. We additionally introduce layer-aligned temporal token propagation to improve semantic consistency across frames, with negligible computational overhead. Across multiple benchmarks, RELO achieves superior results, attaining 57.5% AUC on LaSOText without template updates. This confirms that reward-driven localization provides an effective alternative to prior-driven localization for visual object tracking.

</details>


### [49] [A Marine Debris Detection Framework for Ocean Robots via Self-Attention Enhancement and Feature Interaction Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07388)
*Yuyang Li,Jiashu Han,Yinyi Lai,Wenbin Kang,Zenghui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于海洋机器人检测海洋垃圾的增强型YOLO框架YOLO-MD，通过DB-CASA模块和轻量级移位操作提升低质量图像中的检测性能，并引入SFG-Loss缓解类别不平衡问题，在UODM数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾检测对生态保护至关重要，但实际应用中常受图像模糊、复杂背景和小目标等问题影响，导致检测性能下降。因此需要一种鲁棒性强且高效的检测模型以应对低质量图像中的挑战。

Method: 提出YOLO-MD，包含Dual-Branch Convolutional Enhanced Self-Attention（DB-CASA）模块以增强空间-通道特征交互；引入轻量级基于移位的操作以提升多尺度细粒度特征提取能力；设计SFG-Loss通过动态样本重加权缓解类别不平衡和优化不稳定问题。

Result: 在UODM数据集上，YOLO-MD达到0.875的精确率、0.822的F1分数和0.849的mAP50，优于当前最先进的方法，并通过真实场景下的机器人边缘部署实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: YOLO-MD在处理低质量海洋垃圾图像方面表现出色，具备高精度与良好的泛化能力，适合在资源受限的海洋机器人平台上部署，为海洋生态监测提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Marine debris detection for ocean robot is crucial for ecological protection, yet performance is often degraded by low-quality images with blur, complex backgrounds, and small targets. To address these challenges, we propose YOLO-MD, an enhanced YOLO-based detection framework. A Dual-Branch Convolutional Enhanced Self-Attention (DB-CASA) module is designed to strengthen spatial-channel interactions, improving feature representation in degraded images. Additionally, a lightweight shift-based operation is introduced to enhance fine-grained feature extraction for objects of varying scales while maintaining parameter efficiency. We further propose SFG-Loss to mitigate class imbalance and optimization instability via dynamic sample reweighting. Experiments on the UODM dataset demonstrate that YOLO-MD achieves 0.875 precision, 0.822 F1-score, and 0.849 mAP50, outperforming the latest state-of-the-art methods. The effectiveness of this method has also been verified through real-world robotic edge deployment experiments.

</details>


### [50] [ST-Gen4D: Embedding 4D Spatiotemporal Cognition into World Model for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07390)
*Haonan Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Tao Gu,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四维时空认知的生成框架ST-Gen4D，通过全局外观图与局部动态图的融合实现结构合理且拓扑一致的4D生成，在3D和4D生成任务中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的4D生成模型主要关注全局外观一致性，缺乏对物理世界局部动态的建模能力，难以体现真实的时空规律。因此需要一种能够同时理解全局结构与局部动态的4D生成方法。

Method: 提出ST-Gen4D框架，包含四个关键设计：1）多模态时空表征；2）构建全局外观图与局部动态图，并通过语义桥接融合为4D认知图；3）利用世界模型进行时空推理预测未来状态；4）以推导出的认知为条件指导隐式扩散生成4D高斯表示。

Result: 在多个3D和4D生成任务上进行了广泛实验，结果表明ST-Gen4D在生成内容的结构合理性与拓扑一致性方面优于现有方法。同时构建了新的ST-4D数据集用于评估。

Conclusion: 通过引入4D时空认知机制，将全局结构与局部动态有机结合，显著提升了4D生成的质量与物理合理性，为未来4D内容创作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved success in producing apparently coherent 2D videos, but remain challenging in the physical world due to lack of 4D spatiotemporal scale. Typically, existing 4D generative models directly embed macro scale constraints to enhance overall spatiotemporal consistency. However, these methods only ensure global appearance coherence and fail to reveal the local dynamics of the physical world. Our insight is that global appearance structure and local dynamic topology empower 4D spatiotemporal cognition, thereby enabling 4D generation with spatiotemporal regularities. In this work, we propose ST-Gen4D, a 4D generation framework with 4D spatiotemporal cognition-based world model. Our model is guided by four key designs: 1) Spatiotemporal representation. We encode various modalities into multiple representations as a feature basis. 2) Spatiotemporal cognition. We sculpture these representations into global appearance graph and local dynamic graph, and fuse them via semantic-bridged spatiotemporal fusion to obtain a 4D cognition graph. 3) Spatiotemporal reasoning. We utilize a world model to derive future state based on the 4D cognition. 4) Spatiotemporal generation. We leverage the derived cognition as condition to guide latent diffusion for 4D Gaussian generation. By deeply integrating 4D intrinsic cognition with generative priors, our model guarantees the structural rationality and topological consistency of 4D generation. Moreover, we propose ST-4D datasets by aggregating public 4D datasets and self-built subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our ST-Gen4D across 3D and 4D generation tasks.

</details>


### [51] [BalCapRL: A Balanced Framework for RL-Based MLLM Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07394)
*Shaokai Ye,Vasileios Saveris,Yihao Qian,Jiaming Hu,Elmira Amirloo,Peter Grasch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种更平衡的强化学习框架，用于图像描述生成，联合优化实用性、参考覆盖度和语言质量，并引入了奖励解耦归一化和长度条件奖励掩码技术，在多个基准上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的图像描述方法过于关注单一质量维度，导致在实用性、流畅性和准确性之间出现权衡问题，缺乏对多维度质量的平衡优化。

Method: 提出一种联合优化效用感知正确性、参考覆盖度和语言质量的多目标强化学习框架；采用GDPO风格的奖励解耦归一化处理连续值奖励，并引入长度条件奖励掩码以实现更合理的长度惩罚。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B和Qwen2.5-VL 3B/7B模型上均取得一致提升，DCScore最高提升+13.6，CaptionQA提升+9.0，CapArena提升+29.0。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过多目标平衡优化和改进的奖励机制，有效提升了图像描述的质量与实用性，优于传统RL方法。

Abstract: Image captioning is one of the most fundamental tasks in computer vision. Owing to its open-ended nature, it has received significant attention in the era of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In pursuit of ever more detailed and accurate captions, recent work has increasingly turned to reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing captioning-RL methods and evaluation metrics often emphasize a narrow notion of caption quality, inducing trade-offs across core dimensions of captioning. For example, utility-oriented objectives can encourage noisy, hallucinated, or overlong captions that improve downstream question answering while harming fluency, whereas arena-style objectives can favor fluent but generic descriptions with limited usefulness. To address this, we propose a more balanced RL framework that jointly optimizes utility-aware correctness, reference coverage, and linguistic quality. In order to effectively optimize the resulting continuous multi-objective reward formulation, we apply GDPO-style reward-decoupled normalization to continuous-valued captioning rewards and show that it improves performance over vanilla GRPO. Additionally, we introduce length-conditional reward masking, yielding a more suitable length penalty for captioning. Across LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen2.5-VL 3B and 7B base models, our method consistently improves caption quality, with peak gains of +13.6 DCScore, +9.0 CaptionQA, and +29.0 CapArena across different models.

</details>


### [52] [Exposing and Mitigating Temporal Attack in Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07398)
*Zheyuan Gu,Minghao Shao,Zhen Wang,Yusong Wang,Mingkun Xu,Shijie Zhang,Hao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SpInShield的时空频谱不变防御框架，用于抵御深度伪造视频检测中的逃避攻击，通过学习语义运动与抑制频谱伪影提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空深度伪造检测器容易过拟合于脆弱的时序频谱线索，缺乏对语义因果关系的建模，导致在面对逃避攻击时表现脆弱。

Method: 提出SpInShield框架，引入可学习的频谱对抗模块生成剧烈频谱变形以模拟极端攻击，并采用捷径抑制优化策略，迫使编码器摒弃不稳定的频谱统计特征，提取更可靠的取证线索。

Result: 实验表明，SpInShield在多个常用数据集上表现优异，在模拟幅度频谱攻击下比最强基线模型AUC提升21.30个百分点。

Conclusion: SpInShield能有效解耦语义运动与可操纵的频谱伪影，显著增强检测模型在逃避攻击下的鲁棒性，推动深度伪造检测向更具因果性的建模范式发展。

Abstract: While spatiotemporal deepfake detectors achieve high AUC, our experiments reveal their susceptibility to evasion attacks. These models tend to overfit on fragile temporal spectrum cues, rather than learning robust semantic causality. To mitigate this vulnerability, we propose SpInShield, a temporal spectral-invariant defense framework explicitly designed to decouple semantic motion from manipulatable spectral artifacts. We propose a learnable spectral adversary that dynamically synthesizes severe spectral deformations, simulating extreme attack scenarios. By employing a shortcut suppression optimization strategy, SpInShield compels the encoder to extract reliable forensic cues while purging unstable spectral statistics from the latent space. Experiments show that SpInShield obtains competitive performance on widely used datasets and outperforms the strongest baseline by 21.30 percentage points in AUC under simulated amplitude spectral attacks.

</details>


### [53] [GPO-V: Jailbreak Diffusion Vision Language Model by Global Probability Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07399)
*Yu Pan,Andi Zhang,Yi Wang,Sibei Yang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对扩散视觉-语言模型（dVLMs）的新型越狱攻击方法GPO-V，利用全局概率优化操纵生成动态，揭示了此类模型在安全对齐方面的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管dVLMs看似对传统越狱手段具有鲁棒性，但其独特的拒绝模式暗示存在潜在攻击面，需深入探究其安全性。

Method: 提出Global Probability Optimization (GPO) 及其视觉扩展GPO-V，通过优化去噪轨迹中的全局生成动态而非前缀来绕过安全机制。

Result: 实验表明GPO-V能生成隐蔽且跨模型可迁移性强的扰动，显著突破现有防御。

Conclusion: 扩散型多模态模型存在新型安全威胁，现有防御范式需根本性重估以应对非自回归生成架构的独特风险。

Abstract: Diffusion Vision-Language Models (dVLMs), built upon the non-causal foundations of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), have demonstrated remarkable efficacy in multimodal tasks by departing from the traditional autoregressive generation paradigm. While dVLMs appear inherently robust against conventional jailbreak tactics, which we categorize as Fixed Prefix Optimization (FPO) (e.g., anchoring responses with "Sure, here is"), this perceived resilience is deceptive. Our investigation into the safety landscape of dVLMs reveals a unique refusal pattern: Immediate Refusal and Progressive Refusal. We find that while FPO-based attacks often fail by triggering the latter, the progressive refinement process itself uncovers a novel, latent attack surface. To exploit this vulnerability, we propose Global Probability Optimization (GPO), a general jailbreak paradigm designed specifically for the denoising trajectory of masked diffusion models. Unlike prefix-based methods, GPO manipulates the global generative dynamics to bypass guardrails in diffusion language models. Building on this, we introduce GPO-V, the first visual-modality jailbreak framework tailored for dVLMs. Empirical results demonstrate that GPO-V produces stealthy perturbations with exceptional cross-model transferability, revealing a critical security gap in non-sequential generative architectures. Our findings underscore the critical urgency of addressing safety alignment in dVLMs. These results necessitate an immediate and fundamental re-evaluation of current defense paradigms to mitigate the unique risks of diffusion-based generation. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GPO-V-0250.

</details>


### [54] [InsHuman: Towards Natural and Identity-Preserving Human Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07402)
*Jie Li,Shulian Zhang,Yangyang Gao,Wenbo Li,Yulun Zhang,Yong Guo,Jian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为InsHuman的自然且身份保持的人体插入方法，通过人体-背景自适应融合（HBAF）和面部到面部身份保持（FFIP）技术以及双向数据配对（BDP）策略，解决了现有模型在新背景下人体姿态不当、人数不一致和面部身份改变等问题，并构建了一个高质量的数据集BDP-InsHuman，实验表明该方法在生成合理图像的同时保持了人体身份不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像编辑模型在进行人体插入时常常出现失败情况，如人体姿态不合适、人数不一致及面部身份被修改等；此外，公开可用的人体数据集通常缺乏全身肖像和真实的人体与背景之间的物理交互。因此，需要一种能够自然并保持身份的人体插入方法。

Method: 提出了人体-背景自适应融合（HBAF），检测前景中的人体以获得二值掩码，并应用区域感知加权来对齐预测和真实潜变量之间的人体区域，确保人体的姿态、数量和整体外观能协调地适应目标背景；提出了面部到面部身份保持（FFIP），通过人脸识别特征检测并匹配生成图像和源图像中的面部，以强制每个面部的身份一致性；提出了双向数据配对（BDP）策略，构建了包含真实人体-背景交互的高质量数据集BDP-InsHuman。

Result: 实验结果表明，InsHuman在生成更合理的图像方面取得了显著改进，同时保持了人体身份不变。

Conclusion: InsHuman通过HBAF、FFIP和BDP策略有效解决了人体插入中的关键问题，在保持身份的同时实现了自然的人体插入，为相关领域提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Human insertion aims to naturally place specific individuals into a target background. Although existing image editing models may have such ability, they often produce failure cases, including inappropriate human pose in new background, inconsistent number of people, and modified facial identity. Moreover, publicly available human datasets often lack full-body portraits and realistic physical interaction between humans and their background. To address these challenges, we propose InsHuman for natural and identity-preserving human insertion. Specifically, we propose Human-Background Adaptive Fusion (HBAF), which detects foreground humans to obtain a binary mask and applies region-aware weighting to align the human regions between predicted and ground-truth latents, ensuring the person's pose, count, and overall appearance are coherently adapted to the target background.We further propose Face-to-Face ID-Preserving (FFIP), which detects and matches faces between the generated image and the source image in terms of face recognition features to enforce identity consistency for each face.In addition, we propose Bidirectional Data Pairing (BDP) strategy to construct BDP-InsHuman, a high-quality dataset with realistic human-background interactions. Experiments demonstrate that InsHuman achieves significant improvements in generating plausible images while keeping human identity unchanged.

</details>


### [55] [ChartREG++: Towards Benchmarking and Improving Chart Referring Expression Grounding under Diverse referring clues and Multi-Target Referring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07415)
*Tianhao Niu,Ziyu Han,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的图表指代表达定位基准，解决了现有基准在定位精度、多实例引用、语言线索多样性和图表类型覆盖方面的局限性，并通过代码驱动的合成流程生成像素级精确掩码，提升了多模态大模型在图表理解中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图表指代表达定位基准存在定位形式单一、难以处理多实例引用、过度依赖文本或数据排序线索以及图表类型覆盖有限等问题，限制了视觉-语言模型在图表理解中的发展和评估。

Method: 提出一种代码驱动的合成流程，利用绘图程序与渲染图表元素之间的内在对齐关系，生成跨元素类型和粒度的像素级精确实例掩码；训练一个实例分割模型并将其实例化到通用多模态定位框架中。

Result: 在新提出的基准上，代表性多模态大模型表现出显著性能差距；所提系统在该基准及基于ChartQA的真实图表定位基准上均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文构建的基准和方法有效推动了图表中细粒度空间推理与视觉-语言理解的发展，验证了高精度实例掩码与多模态框架结合在复杂图表指代表达定位任务中的优势。

Abstract: Referring expression grounding is a core problem in visual grounding and is widely used as a diagnostic of spatial grounding and reasoning in vision and language models, yet most prior work focuses on natural images. In contrast, existing chart referring expression grounding-related benchmarks remain limited: (1) they largely adopt bounding boxes, constraining localization precision for fine chart elements (2) they mostly assume a single and two referred target instances, failing to handle multi-instance target references; (3) the language expressions over-rely on textual cues or data-rank clues (4) they cover only a narrow range of chart types. To address these issues, we introduce a chart referring expression grounding benchmark that systematically supports multiple localization forms, multiple referred targets, diverse grounding cues and diverse chart types. Results across representative multimodal large models reveal a significant performance gap. We further introduce a code-driven synthesis pipeline that exploits the inherent alignment between plotting programs and rendered chart primitives to derive pixel accurate instance masks across chart element types and granularities. We train an instance segmentation model with the synthesized masks and integrate it into a general-purpose multimodal grounding framework. The resulting system consistently outperforms baselines on our benchmark and generalizes well to a ChartQA-derived real-chart grounding benchmark.

</details>

### [56] [Domain-level metacognitive monitoring in frontier LLMs: A 33-model atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06673)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了33个前沿大语言模型在MMLU基准测试中的元认知监控表现，发现尽管整体聚合分数具有误导性，但所有模型在不同知识领域间存在显著的元认知质量差异，其中应用/专业类知识最易监控，而形式推理与自然科学最难，建议在部署前进行领域特异性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合的元认知质量评分可能掩盖模型在不同知识领域间的实际表现差异，研究旨在揭示这种被忽略的内部变异，并检验当前MMLU六领域分类的有效性与实用性。

Method: 对来自8个模型家族的33个前沿LLM进行1,500个MMLU项目测试（每领域250项），使用言语化置信度（0-100）计算每个模型-领域的二型AUROC，共47,151次观测；采用Kendall's W、聚类分析、重测稳定性与内部一致性等方法分析数据。

Result: 所有具备高于随机水平的整体监控能力的模型均表现出显著的领域级变异；应用/专业知识最易监控（平均AUROC=0.742，33个模型中21个位列前二），形式推理与自然科学最难（33个模型中27个位列后二）；中间三个领域无统计差异（Kendall's W = 0.164）；六领域分组具实践合理性但非潜在构念；Anthropic、Google-Gemini和Qwen家族内轮廓聚类显著；Gemma 4 31B较前代提升+0.202 AUROC；三种在二元探针中无效的模型在言语化信心下表现正常，显示探针格式特异性；剖面级重测信度较低（平均r=0.184）。

Conclusion: 聚合指标会掩盖稳定的领域级元认知差异，研究支持在将模型部署至特定应用场景前，应进行基于领域的筛查与评估，以更准确地反映其实际可用性。

Abstract: Aggregate metacognitive quality scores mask within-model variation across MMLU benchmark domains. We administered 1,500 MMLU items (250 per domain, under an a priori six-domain grouping) to 33 frontier LLMs from eight model families and computed Type-2 AUROC per model-domain cell using verbalized confidence (0-100). Total observations: 47,151. Every model with above-chance aggregate monitoring showed non-trivial domain-level variation. Applied/Professional knowledge was reliably the easiest benchmark domain to monitor (mean AUROC = .742, ranked top-2 in 21 of 33 models); Formal Reasoning and Natural Science were reliably the hardest (one of the two ranked bottom-2 in 27 of 33 models). The three middle domains were statistically indistinguishable (Kendall's W = .164). A subject-level coherence analysis (within-domain similarity ratio = 0.95) confirms the six-domain grouping is a pragmatic benchmark taxonomy, not a validated latent construct. Within-family profile-shape clustering is significant for Anthropic, Google-Gemini, and Qwen (permutation p < .0001) but not DeepSeek, Google-Gemma, or OpenAI. Gemma 4 31B showed a +.202 AUROC improvement over Gemma 3 27B. Three models classified Invalid on binary KEEP/WITHDRAW probes produced normal profiles under verbalized confidence, confirming probe-format specificity. Bootstrap 95% CIs on 198 cells have median width .199. Split-half aggregate stability r = .893; profile-level split-half is weaker (grand median r = .184). These results show stable benchmark-domain variation obscured by aggregate metrics, and support benchmark-stage domain screening as a step before deployment in specific application areas.

</details>


### [57] [VITA-QinYu: Expressive Spoken Language Model for Role-Playing and Singing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06765)
*Jiacheng Xu,Heting Gao,Liufei Xie,Zhenchuan Yang,Lijiang Li,Yiting Chen,Bin Zhang,Meng Chen,Chaoyu Fu,Weifeng Zhao,Wenjiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VITA-QinYu是首个支持角色扮演和歌唱生成的端到端表达性语音语言模型，采用混合语音-文本范式和多码本音频标记，在表达性与对话质量上均达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了突破传统语音语言模型仅限于自然对话的局限，实现更丰富的表达性（如角色扮演和歌唱），需要构建能处理复杂副语言信息并保持模态清晰分离的模型。

Method: 提出VITA-QinYu模型，采用混合语音-文本建模范式，引入多码本音频标记以增强副语言表征，并构建包含15.8K小时数据的数据生成 pipeline 用于训练。

Result: 在角色扮演任务上比同类模型高7个百分点，在歌唱MOS评分上高出0.13分；同时在C3和URO对话基准上分别提升1.38和4.98个百分点，达到最优性能。

Conclusion: VITA-QinYu实现了在角色扮演、歌唱和自然对话三方面兼顾的高质量语音生成，推动了表达性语音语言模型的发展，且已开源代码与模型并提供全栈式交互演示。

Abstract: Human speech conveys expressiveness beyond linguistic content, including personality, mood, or performance elements, such as a comforting tone or humming a song, which we formalize as role-playing and singing. We present VITA-QinYu, the first expressive end-to-end (E2E) spoken language model (SLM) that goes beyond natural conversation to support both role-playing and singing generation. VITA-QinYu adopts a hybrid speech-text paradigm that extends interleaved text-audio modeling with multi-codebook audio tokens, a design enabling richer paralinguistic representation while preserving a clear separation between modalities to avoid interference. We further develop a comprehensive data generation pipeline to synthesize a total of 15.8K hours of natural conversation, role-playing, and singing data for training. VITA-QinYu demonstrates superior expressiveness, outperforming peer SLMs by 7 percentage points on objective role-playing benchmarks, and surpassing peer models by 0.13 points on a 5-point MOS scale for singing. Simultaneously, it achieves state-of-the-art conversational accuracy and fluency, exceeding prior SLMs by 1.38 and 4.98 percentage points on the C3 and URO benchmarks, respectively. We open-source our code and models and provide an easy-to-use demo with full-stack support for streaming and full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [58] [IntentGrasp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Intent Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06832)
*Yuwei Yin,Chuyuan Li,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IntentGrasp，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）意图理解能力的综合基准，并提出Intentional Fine-Tuning（IFT）方法显著提升模型表现，展示了跨领域泛化能力，为构建更智能、安全的AI助手提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确理解语音、对话和文本背后的意图对开发有用的大型语言模型助手至关重要，但现有模型在多样化真实场景中的意图理解能力尚不理想，亟需系统性评估与提升方法。

Method: 构建了涵盖12个领域的高质量基准IntentGrasp，包含26万多个训练样本和两个测试集（All Set和Gem Set），并对20个主流大模型进行评测；提出Intentional Fine-Tuning（IFT）方法，在IntentGrasp训练集上微调模型以增强意图理解能力，并通过leave-one-domain-out实验验证其跨域泛化性。

Result: 20个大模型在All Set上的性能低于60%，在更具挑战性的Gem Set上低于25%；17个模型在Gem Set上甚至不如随机猜测（15.2%），而人类表现约为81.1%；采用IFT后，模型在All Set上提升超30 F1点，Gem Set上提升超20点，且具备良好跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在复杂意图理解任务上仍有显著不足，IntentGrasp为评估该能力提供了可靠基准，IFT则提供了一种有效提升路径，整体推动了更智能、安全的人工智能助手的发展。

Abstract: Accurately understanding the intent behind speech, conversation, and writing is crucial to the development of helpful Large Language Model (LLM) assistants. This paper introduces IntentGrasp, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the intent understanding capability of LLMs. Derived from 49 high-quality, open-licensed corpora spanning 12 diverse domains, IntentGrasp is constructed through source datasets curation, intent label contextualization, and task format unification. IntentGrasp contains a large-scale training set of 262,759 instances and two evaluation sets: an All Set of 12,909 test cases and a more balanced and challenging Gem Set of 470 cases. Extensive evaluations on 20 LLMs across 7 families (including frontier models such as GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and Claude-Opus-4.7) demonstrate unsatisfactory performance, with scores below 60% on All Set and below 25% on Gem set. Notably, 17 out of 20 tested models perform worse than a random-guess baseline (15.2%) on Gem Set, while the estimated human performance is ~81.1%, showing substantial room for improvement. To enhance such ability, this paper proposes Intentional Fine-Tuning (IFT), which fine-tunes the models on the training set in IntentGrasp, yielding significant gains of 30+ F1 points on All Set and 20+ points on Gem Set. Tellingly, the leave-one-domain-out (Lodo) experiments further demonstrate the strong cross-domain generalizability of IFT, verifying that it is a promising approach to substantially enhancing the intent understanding of LLMs. Overall, by benchmarking and boosting intent understanding ability, this study sheds light on a promising path towards more intentional, capable, and safe AI assistants for human benefits and social good.

</details>


### [59] [TajPersLexon: A Tajik-Persian Lexical Resource and Hybrid Model for Cross-Script Low-Resource NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06886)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TajPersLexon，一个包含40,112个词和短语对的塔吉克-波斯平行词汇资源，并比较了三种方法在低资源环境下的表现，发现神经和检索方法能达到98-99%的top-1准确率，而轻量级混合模型在实际应用中具有良好的准确性和效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在低资源环境下实现跨脚本词汇检索、音译和对齐，构建一个高质量的塔吉克-波斯平行词汇资源并评估不同方法的有效性。

Method: 提出了TajPersLexon资源，并在仅使用CPU的条件下对三种方法进行基准测试：轻量级混合流水线、神经序列到序列模型和检索方法。

Result: 神经和检索方法在任务上达到98-99%的top-1准确率，表明任务基本可解；大型多语言句子变换器在此精确词汇匹配任务上失败，而混合模型在OCR后纠错任务中达到96.4%的准确率。

Conclusion: 尽管大型模型表现不佳，但所提出的可解释混合模型在准确性与效率之间提供了良好权衡，适合实际应用，且所有数据、代码和模型将公开发布以确保可复现性。

Abstract: This work introduces TajPersLexon, a curated Tajik--Persian parallel lexical resource of 40,112 word and short-phrase pairs for cross-script lexical retrieval, transliteration, and alignment in low-resource settings. We conduct a comprehensive CPU-only benchmark comparing three methodological families: (i) a lightweight hybrid pipeline, (ii) neural sequence-to-sequence models, and (iii) retrieval methods. Our evaluation establishes that the task is essentially solvable, with neural and retrieval baselines achieving 98-99% top-1 accuracy. Crucially, we demonstrate that while large multilingual sentence transformers fail on this exact lexical matching, our interpretable hybrid model offers a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for practical applications, achieving 96.4% accuracy in an OCR post-correction task. All experiments use fixed random seeds for full reproducibility. The dataset, code, and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [60] [MIST: Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Conversational Assistants for Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06897)
*Maximillian Chen,Xuanming Zhang,Michael Peng,Zhou Yu,Alexandros Papangelis,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIST，一个用于语音驱动的多模态交互式工具调用的数据集，旨在解决物联网设备中复杂用户交互的建模挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在处理真实世界物联网设备时面临时空约束、语音输入、动态状态跟踪和混合主动交互模式等挑战，缺乏专门的数据集支持相关研究。

Method: 构建了一个名为MIST的合成多轮语音驱动代码生成任务数据集，并开发了可扩展的数据生成框架，以支持对物理世界约束下语音助手的研究。

Result: 实验发现开源与闭源多模态大模型在MIST上均表现不佳，尤其是前沿闭源模型仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: MIST为研究面向物联网的混合主动语音助手提供了新的基准和工具，推动了该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: The rise of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in the physical world necessitates voice-based interfaces capable of handling complex user experiences. While modern Large Language Models (LLMs) already demonstrate strong tool-usage capabilities, modeling real-world IoT devices presents a difficult, understudied challenge which combines modeling spatiotemporal constraints with speech inputs, dynamic state tracking, and mixed-initiative interaction patterns. We introduce MIST (the Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Dataset), a synthetic multi-turn, voice-driven code generation task that operates over IoT devices. We find that there is a significant gap between open- and closed-weight multimodal LLMs on MIST, and that even frontier closed-weight LLMs have substantial headroom. We release MIST and an extensible data generation framework to build related datasets in order to facilitate research on mixed-initiative voice assistants which reason about physical world constraints.

</details>


### [61] [Reflections and New Directions for Human-Centered Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06901)
*Caleb Ziems,Dora Zhao,Rose E. Wang,Matthew Jörke,Ahmad Rushdi,Advit Deepak,Sunny Yu,Anshika Agarwal,Harshvardhan Agarwal,Gabriela Aranguiz-Dias,Aditri Bhagirath,Justine Breuch,Huanxing Chen,Ruishi Chen,Sarah Chen,Haocheng Fan,William Fang,Cat Gonzales Fergesen,Daniel Frees,Tian Gao,Ziqing Huang,Vishal Jain,Yucheng Jiang,Kirill Kalinin,Su Doga Karaca,Arpandeep Khatua,Teland La,Isabelle Levent,Miranda Li,Xinling Li,Yongce Li,Angela Liu,Minsik Oh,Nathan J. Paek,Anthony Qin,Emily Redmond,Michael J. Ryan,Aadesh Salecha,Xiaoxian Shen,Pranava Singhal,Shashanka Subrahmanya,Mei Tan,Irawadee Thawornbut,Michelle Vinocour,Xiaoyue Wang,Zheng Wang,Henry Jin Weng,Pawan Wirawarn,Shirley Wu,Sophie Wu,Yichen Xie,Patrick Ye,Sean Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Cathy Zhou,Yiling Zhao,James Landay,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以人为本的大型语言模型（HCLLM）开发框架，整合了自然语言处理、人机交互和负责任的人工智能视角，强调在模型开发各阶段系统性地考虑人类关切、偏好和价值观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型对社会影响的增加，亟需在技术发展的同时优先考虑伦理、经济和人类价值，避免仅在训练后阶段简单处理人类因素。

Method: 结合自然语言处理、人机交互和负责任AI的跨学科方法，构建覆盖系统设计、数据获取、模型训练、评估与部署全流程的HCLLM框架。

Result: 提出了在每个开发阶段融入人类中心理念的具体见解与建议，并通过一个关于HCLLM未来工作场景的案例研究验证了该框架的应用价值。

Conclusion: 开发大型语言模型应系统性地将人类需求和价值观嵌入整个技术 pipeline，而不仅限于后期调整，HCLLM框架有助于实现更负责任和可持续的AI发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping the private and professional lives of users, with numerous applications in business, education, finance, healthcare, law, and science. With this rise in global influence comes greater urgency to build, evaluate, and deploy these systems in a manner that prioritizes not only technical capabilities but also human priorities. This work presents a framework for developing Human-Centered Large Language Models (HCLLMs), which integrates perspectives from Natural Language Processing (NLP), Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), and responsible AI. Considering the ethics, economics, and technical objectives of language modeling, we argue that model developers need to address human concerns, preferences, values, and goals, not only during a cursory post-training stage, but rather with rigor and care at every stage of the pipeline. This paper offers human-centered insights and recommendations for developers at each stage, from system design to data sourcing, model training, evaluation, and responsible deployment. Then we conclude with a case study, applying these insights to understand the future of work with HCLLMs.

</details>


### [62] [MELD: Multi-Task Equilibrated Learning Detector for AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06903)
*Chenjun Li,Cheng Wan,Johannes C. Paetzold*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MELD，一种多任务均衡学习的AI生成文本检测器，通过引入生成器家族、攻击类型和来源领域的辅助监督，并结合不确定性加权、知识蒸馏与难负样本排序损失，在鲁棒性、跨域迁移性和低误报率下表现优异，是当前最强的开源检测器之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测器多聚焦于二分类任务，缺乏对生成模型、攻击手段和领域变化的鲁棒性，难以应对实际应用中的对抗重写、未知生成器和低误报需求。因此需要一种更具泛化性和鲁棒性的检测方法。

Method: 提出MELD框架：1）在共享编码器上附加生成器家族、攻击类型、来源领域三个辅助任务头，形成四任务联合学习；2）使用同方差不确定性自动平衡各任务损失权重；3）采用EMA教师模型在干净文本上预测，学生模型通过对抗增强进行蒸馏学习；4）引入难负样本对排序损失，扩大AI文本与最易混淆人类文本之间的得分差距；5）推理时丢弃所有辅助头，保持与标准检测器一致的接口和成本。

Result: 在公开RAID排行榜上，MELD是性能最强的开源检测器，且与商业模型相当，尤其在遭受攻击和低误报率场景下表现突出；在多个标准基准测试中，性能达到或超过有监督基线；在新构建的MELD-eval（包含四大LLM厂商最新对话模型）上，无需微调即达到99.9%真正率（TPR）在1%误报率（FPR）下，而多数基线显著下降。

Conclusion: MELD通过多任务学习与鲁棒训练策略，显著提升了AI生成文本检测的泛化能力与实用性，尤其在低误报、抗攻击和跨模型场景下优势明显，为部署级检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are now embedded in everyday writing workflows, making reliable AI-generated text detection important for academic integrity, content moderation, and provenance tracking. In practice, however, a detector must do more than achieve high aggregate AUROC on clean, in-distribution human and AI text: it should remain robust to attacks and adversarial rewrites, transfer to unseen generators and domains, and operate at low false-positive rates (FPR). Most existing detectors optimize a single AI/Human objective, giving the representation little incentive to learn generator, attack, or domain structure once the binary task saturates. We introduce MELD (Multi-Task Equilibrated Learning Detector), a deployable detector for AI-generated text that enriches binary detection with auxiliary supervision. MELD attaches generator-family, attack-type, and source-domain heads to a shared encoder, and balances the four losses with learned homoscedastic uncertainty weights. To improve robustness, an EMA teacher predicts on clean inputs while an attack-augmented student is distilled toward the teacher. MELD further uses a hard-negative pairwise ranking loss to enlarge the score margin between AI-generated texts and the most confusable human texts. At inference, all auxiliary heads are discarded, giving MELD the same interface and cost as a standard detector. On the public RAID leaderboard, MELD is the strongest open-source detector and is competitive with leading commercial models, especially under attack and at low FPR. Across standard held-out benchmarks, MELD matches or outperforms supervised baselines. We further introduce MELD-eval, a held-out evaluation pool built from recent chat models released by four major LLM providers. Without additional finetuning, MELD achieves 99.9% TPR at 1% FPR on MELD-eval, while many baselines degrade sharply.

</details>


### [63] [Can LLMs Take Retrieved Information with a Grain of Salt?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06919)
*Behzad Shayegh,Mohamed Osama Ahmed,Fred Tung,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了八种大语言模型在检索信息确定性变化时调整回答的能力，发现模型存在对不确定性上下文处理不当的问题，并提出一种无需修改模型权重的交互策略，显著提升其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在高风险领域（如医学和金融）的应用中，需能根据检索信息的确定性调整回答，但当前对此能力的研究尚不足。

Method: 通过评估八种大语言模型在不同确定性上下文下的响应表现，分析其在记忆保持、确定性解读和上下文信任方面的缺陷，并提出结合先验提醒、确定性再校准和上下文简化的交互策略。

Result: 发现大语言模型普遍存在对不确定上下文适应不良的问题，所提交互策略平均减少25%的服从错误。

Conclusion: 通过合理的交互设计可有效提升大语言模型对上下文确定性的遵从能力，增强其在高风险场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated impressive retrieval-augmented capabilities. However, a crucial area remains underexplored: their ability to appropriately adapt responses to the certainty of the retrieved information. It is a limitation with real consequences in high-stakes domains like medicine and finance. We evaluate eight LLMs on their context-certainty obedience, measuring how well they adjust responses to match expressed context certainty. Our analysis reveals systematic limitations: LLMs struggle to recall prior knowledge after observing an uncertain context, misinterpret expressed certainties, and overtrust complex contexts. To address these, we propose an interaction strategy combining prior reminders, certainty recalibration, and context simplification. This approach reduces obedience errors by 25% on average, without modifying model weights, demonstrating the efficacy of interaction design in enhancing LLM reliability. Our contributions include a principled evaluation metric, empirical insights into LLMs' uncertainty handling, and a portable strategy to improve context-certainty obedience across diverse LLMs.

</details>


### [64] [MultiSoc-4D: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Instruction-Induced Label Collapse in Closed-Set LLM Annotation of Bengali Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06940)
*Souvik Pramanik,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Shak Mohammad Abyad,Md. Ibrahim Khalil,Md. Shahriar Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiSoc-4D，一个用于检测孟加拉语NLP数据集中标注偏差的基准数据集，并揭示了大语言模型在封闭指令下存在的“指令诱导标签坍塌”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型（LLMs）在低资源语言中对封闭集合指令的标注行为，尤其是标注自动化中的潜在偏差问题。

Method: 构建了一个包含58K+条评论的孟加拉语社交媒体数据集MultiSoc-4D，涵盖四个标注维度；使用ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude和Grok等模型分别标注不同分区，并共享20%的验证集以系统分析LLM行为。

Result: 发现LLMs普遍存在‘指令诱导标签坍塌’现象，倾向于选择默认标签（如Other、Neutral、No），导致少数类别被严重低估，例如对仇恨言论和讽刺内容的漏检率分别达79%和75%；讽刺检测的Fleiss' Kappa接近于零，显示无实际标注一致性。在40多个LLM中验证了该偏差在训练流程中的传播。

Conclusion: ‘标签坍塌’会导致高表观一致性和低实际质量之间的错觉，即‘标签一致性的幻觉’；MultiSoc-4D可用于诊断低资源语言中LLM标注的系统性偏差。

Abstract: Annotation automation via Large Language Models (LLMs) is the core approach for scaling NLP datasets; however, LLM behavior with respect to closed-set instructions in low-resource languages has not been well studied. We present MultiSoc-4D, a Bengali social media dataset benchmark, which contains 58K+ social media comments from six sources annotated along four dimensions: category, sentiment, hate speech, and sarcasm. By employing a structured pipeline where ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Grok individually annotate separate partitions, while sharing a common validation set of 20%, we diagnose LLM behavior systematically. We discover a prevalent phenomenon called "instruction-induced label collapse", wherein LLMs show a systematic preference towards fallback labels (Other, Neutral, No), leading to high agreement rates but under-detection of minority categories. For example, we find that LLMs failed to detect 79% and 75% of instances with hateful and sarcastic content compared to a human-calibrated reference. Furthermore, we prove that it represents a "label agreement illusion", statistically validated via almost null Fleiss' Kappa ($κ\approx -0.001$) on sarcasm detection. Across 40+ LLMs, we benchmark this annotation bias propagation within the training pipeline, regardless of architectural differences. We release MultiSoc-4D as a diagnostic benchmark for annotation biases in Bengali NLP.

</details>


### [65] [Towards Closing the Autoregressive Gap in Language Modeling via Entropy-Gated Continuous Bitstream Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07013)
*Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于固定宽度二进制比特流的连续扩散语言模型，通过模拟文本的连续扩散过程，在生成质量与效率上显著提升，缩小了与自回归模型的差距，并解决了传统离散扩散模型的词汇量扩展瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在生成质量和多样性方面长期落后于自回归模型，且存在词汇量扩展瓶颈，本文旨在通过连续状态空间和比特流表示来克服这些问题。

Method: 将文本建模为固定宽度二进制比特流上的连续扩散过程，采用匹配滤波残差参数化方法，并设计一种基于熵率分布的随机采样器（Langevin-type校正），集中对高信息区域进行随机优化。

Result: 在LM1B数据集上，130M参数模型以256次神经函数评估达到GenPPL=59.76；在OWT上以更少步数实现GenPPL=27.06，性能超越先前DLM基线并逼近自回归模型，同时内存占用更低、吞吐更高。

Conclusion: 比特流扩散提供了一种高效、可扩展的语言生成范式，有效结合了连续建模优势与低复杂度推理，为未来大词汇量场景下的语言模型设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) promise parallel, order-agnostic generation, but on standard benchmarks they have historically lagged behind autoregressive models in sample quality and diversity. Recent continuous flow and diffusion approaches over token embeddings have narrowed this gap, suggesting continuous state spaces are highly effective for language. In this work, we further close the autoregressive gap by modeling text as a continuous diffusion process over fixed-width binary bitstreams. Our approach represents semantic tokens as analog bit sequences and utilizes a matched-filter residual parameterization to isolate contextual learning from analytic independent-bit posteriors. Crucially, we adopt a stochastic sampler that applies Langevin-type corrections gated by the entropy-rate profile, automatically concentrating stochasticity in high-information regions while remaining nearly deterministic elsewhere. On the One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B), our 130M-parameter bitstream model reaches a generative perplexity ($\GenPPL$) of $59.76$ at matched real-data entropy ($4.31$) using 256 neural function evaluations (NFEs), decisively outperforming prior DLM baselines and reaching the autoregressive reference. On OpenWebText (OWT), our stochastic sampler establishes a new continuous-DLM Pareto frontier, achieving $\GenPPL=27.06$ at an entropy of $5.26$ using $4\times$ fewer steps than previous 1024-NFE baselines. As an additional architectural benefit, bitstream diffusion removes the $\mathcal{O}(V)$ vocabulary scaling bottleneck shared by standard DLMs. By predicting $\mathcal{O}(\log V)$ bitwise logits via semantic bit-patching, our model yields a reduced memory footprint and higher throughput, demonstrating a scalable paradigm for language generation as vocabulary sizes grow.

</details>


### [66] [MedExAgent: Training LLM Agents to Ask, Examine, and Diagnose in Noisy Clinical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07058)
*Yicheng Gao,Xiaolin Zhou,Yahan Li,Yue Zhao,Ruishan Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将临床诊断建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），并构建包含多种噪声类型的系统化环境，训练出高效、低成本的诊断智能体MedExAgent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大模型和自动诊断方法大多简化了真实临床诊断的复杂性，忽略了交互性和不确定性，无法反映实际诊疗过程。

Method: 将临床诊断形式化为包含三种动作类型的POMDP框架（提问、检查、诊断），引入七类患者噪声和三类检查噪声；采用两阶段训练：先基于Calgary-Cambridge模型进行监督微调，再通过DAPO优化综合奖励（准确性、工具质量、检查成本）。

Result: MedExAgent在诊断性能上可与更大模型媲美，同时保持更优的检查成本控制（包括经济成本和患者不适）；实验和消融研究验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作更贴近真实临床场景，为医学LLM提供了更具挑战性和现实意义的评估与训练框架。

Abstract: Real-world clinical diagnosis is a complex process in which the doctor is required to obtain information from both interaction with the patient and conducting medical exams. Additionally, the doctor needs to adapt to different patient personas, as well as noisy and incomplete information that can happen at any time during the process. However, existing benchmarks for medical LLMs and methods for automatic diagnosis largely simplify this process by reducing it to single-turn question answering, noise-free conversations, or sequential exam making, etc., ignoring the interactive and uncertain nature of clinical diagnosis. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by formalizing clinical diagnosis as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) with three action types: questioning the patient, ordering medical exams as tool calls, and issuing a diagnosis. We also introduce a systematic noise model comprising seven patient noise types and three exam noise types. Using our proposed environment, we train an effective diagnosis agent, \textbf{MedExAgent}, through a two-stage pipeline that first performs supervised finetuning on synthetic conversations structured after the Calgary-Cambridge model for clinical interviews, and then applies DAPO to optimize a composite reward capturing diagnostic accuracy, tool call quality, and exam cost including financial cost and patient discomfort. Through extensive experiments and ablation studies, we demonstrate that MedExAgent achieves diagnostic performance comparable to larger models while maintaining cost-efficient examination strategies.

</details>


### [67] [WiCER: Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine Iterative Knowledge Compilation for LLM Wiki Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07068)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为WiCER的迭代算法，用于解决将原始文档编译成维基知识库时的关键信息丢失问题（即“编译鸿沟”），通过诊断探测和反例引导的方式提升大语言模型在领域知识应用中的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决LLM Wiki模式中因盲目编译导致关键事实大量丢失的问题，尤其是在大规模应用场景下注意力稀释使性能下降的挑战。

Method: 提出WiCER（Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine）算法，受CEGAR启发，通过评估已编译的维基知识、识别遗漏的关键事实，并强制在后续编译中保留这些信息，实现迭代优化。

Result: 在17个RepLiQA领域中验证，经过一到两次迭代后恢复了80%的性能损失，灾难性失败率相对降低55%，且诊断驱动的改进显著优于通用固定方法。

Conclusion: WiCER有效缩小了LLM Wiki模式中的编译鸿沟，提升了知识编译的质量与可靠性，推动了基于KV缓存推理的高效低延迟上下文访问的实用化。

Abstract: The LLM Wiki pattern, to compile and provide domain knowledge into a persistent artifact and serve it to LLMs via KV cache inference, promises context access at sub-second latency with zero retrieval failure. Realizing this requires solving the compilation gap: LLM compilation distilling raw documents into a wiki without catastrophically discarding critical facts. We characterize this gap across 17 RepLiQA domains (6,800 questions): we observe that full context KV cache inference outperforms RAG on curated knowledge (4.38 vs. 4.08 out of 5, 7.3 faster TTFT) but degrades below RAG at scale due to attention dilution, and blind compilation fails entirely (2.14 to 2.32 vs. 3.46, 53 to 60% catastrophic failure rate). To address the compilation gap, we propose WiCER (Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine), an iterative algorithm inspired by counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) that closes this gap. WiCER evaluates compiled wikis against diagnostic probes, identifies dropped facts, and forces their preservation in subsequent compilations. One to two iterations recover 80% of lost quality (mean 3.24 vs. 3.47 for raw full-context across the 15 topics with baselines), reducing catastrophic failures by 55% relative. An ablation across all 17 topics confirms that targeted diagnosis (+0.95), not generic pinning (+0.16), drives the gains. All code and benchmarks are released for reproducible research.

</details>


### [68] [Self-Consolidating Language Models: Continual Knowledge Incorporation from Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07076)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Zihan Dong,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCoL框架，通过元强化学习实现大语言模型在流式上下文中的持续知识整合，提升信息获取与保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文窗口虽提供更多信息，但无法保证有用信息被有效保留和复用，需解决持续上下文整合问题。

Method: 提出Self-Consolidating Language Models (SCoL)，利用元强化学习训练模型生成文本更新指令，动态选择Transformer层进行权重更新。

Result: 在SQuAD和LongBench v2任务上，SCoL在知识吸收与保持方面优于提示、摘要、测试时批量训练和顺序微调等基线方法。

Conclusion: SCoL能有效引导模型将可塑性集中在对损失敏感的高Fisher信息层，减少干扰，并具备从短序列训练向长序列推理迁移的能力，支持可扩展的流式知识整合。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly receive information as streams of passages, conversations, and long-context workflows. While longer context windows expose more evidence, they do not ensure that useful information is preserved and reused. We study continual context consolidation: writing current context into model weights while limiting interference with previously consolidated information. We propose \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{Co}nsolidating \textbf{L}anguage Models (SCoL), a post-training framework in which, given current context, an LLM learns to generate textual update instructions specifying which of its own Transformer layers should be updated. Because committed updates change the model that later generates future selections, we train SCoL with meta-reinforcement learning over an evolving model state. We instantiate SCoL with supervised QA rewards on SQuAD knowledge incorporation and intrinsic likelihood-based rewards for LongBench v2 long-context consolidation. Across both settings, SCoL improves acquisition and retention over prompting, summarization, batch test-time training, and sequential finetuning baselines. Analysis of learned selection patterns shows that SCoL encourages the LLM to generate sparse update locations that align with layers of high Fisher information, suggesting that the model learns to route plasticity toward loss-sensitive regions while limiting interference. Moreover, SCoL transfers from shorter meta-training streams to longer LongBench v2 streams at evaluation, suggesting that our framework supports scalable streaming consolidation.

</details>


### [69] [Beyond Single Ground Truth: Reference Monism as Epistemic Injustice in ASR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07084)
*Anna Seo Gyeong Choi,Maria Teleki,James Caverlee,Miguel del Rio,Corey Miller,Hoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了自动语音识别（ASR）评估中使用单一转录规范作为“真实参考”的问题，指出这种“参考单一主义”会对失语症患者造成认知不公。作者提出“解释性鸿沟”概念和“认知不公距离”（EID）度量，并建议采用WER-Range方法，在多种合理转录规范下报告ASR性能，以实现更公平的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ASR评估依赖于单一的转录规范作为地面真值，忽视了不同说话者（如失语症患者）语言特征的多样性，导致对其表现的系统性误判和边缘化，构成认知不公。

Method: 构建哲学框架引入“解释性鸿沟”概念，形式化定义“认知不公距离”（EID）来量化参考单一主义的成本，并利用AphasiaBank数据集实证展示不同转录规范下WER的变化。

Result: 实验证明，根据所采用的转录规范不同，同一ASR输出的WER存在显著差异；使用非逐字或临床相关规范时，对失语症患者的评估更为公正。

Conclusion: 应摒弃将单一转录规范视为绝对标准的做法，提倡使用WER-Range报告在多种合法转录惯例下的性能，以提升ASR评估的公平性和包容性。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) evaluation compares system output to ground truth transcripts, with Word Error Rate (WER) quantifying the distance between them. But ground truth transcripts are not discovered - they are produced by human annotators following conventions that encode normative assumptions about which speech features matter. Different conventions (verbatim, non-verbatim, legal) produce different transcripts of identical speech and judge the same ASR output differently. This paper argues that reference monism - enforcing a single transcription convention as ground truth - commits epistemic injustice. Speakers with aphasia, whose speech includes clinically meaningful disfluencies, are systematically disadvantaged when evaluated against "clean" references that treat those disfluencies as errors. The harm is not merely differential performance, but that evaluative infrastructure lacks interpretive resources to recognize their contributions as legitimate. We develop a philosophical framework introducing the hermeneutical gap, formalize Epistemic Injustice Distance (EID) to measure reference monism's cost, and demonstrate empirically using AphasiaBank that WER varies depending on which convention defines ground truth. We propose WER-Range: reporting performance across legitimate conventions rather than assuming a single correct answer.

</details>


### [70] [SAGE: Hierarchical LLM-Based Literary Evaluation through Ontology-Grounded Interpretive Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07102)
*Tianyu Wang,Nianjun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SAGE的分层评估框架，用于通过基于本体的解释维度和结构化的大语言模型评估来衡量文学质量，在600次评估中表现出高可靠性，并揭示了当前生成模型在文化批判和哲学深度方面与人类文学创作之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学质量涉及文化表征、情感深度和哲学复杂性等难以用传统计算方法量化的解释性维度，因此需要一种可靠且理论驱动的自动化评估方法。

Method: 提出了SAGE框架，将文学质量分解为三个分析层次（文化、情感-心理、存在-哲学），采用双模式评估（基于内容和基于元数据）并结合多轮迭代反思与独立验证的大语言模型进行结构化评估。

Result: 在100篇短篇小说（包括经典、通俗小说和LLM生成文本）上的实验显示，该框架达到98.8%的评分收敛率和超过94%的评分者间一致性，且两种评估模式之间具有近完美的不变性；统计分析发现文体间存在显著等级差异（经典 > 通俗 > LLM，均p<0.001），其中文化批判与哲学深度效应量极大（Cohen's d>2.4），情感表达差距较小（d=1.68）；跨层相关性分析（r=0.649–0.683）表明三维度可区分但相关。

Conclusion: SAGE框架展示了理论引导下的大语言模型评估可实现测量级的可靠性，能系统识别生成模型在关键文学维度上的不足，为开放式文本生成的可扩展自动评估提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Evaluating literary quality requires assessing interpretive dimensions such as cultural representation, emotional depth, and philosophical sophistication that resist straightforward computational measurement. We introduce SAGE, a hierarchical evaluation framework that decomposes literary quality into ontology-grounded interpretive dimensions assessed through structured large language model evaluation with multi-round iterative reflection and independent validation. We validate the framework on 100 short stories (50 canonical works, 30 pulp fiction, 20 LLM-generated narratives) across three analytical layers (cultural, emotional-psychological, existential-philosophical) using dual-mode assessment. Across 600 evaluations, the framework achieves 98.8% score convergence and greater than 94% inter-rater agreement, with near-perfect mode invariance between content-based and metadata-based evaluation. Statistical analysis reveals a consistent genre hierarchy (Canonical > Pulp > LLM, all p<0.001) with layer-specific discrimination: cultural critique and philosophical depth exhibit very large effect sizes (Cohen's d>2.4), while emotional representation shows smaller gaps (d=1.68), suggesting that affective patterns are more learnable from training data than critical stance or philosophical depth. Cross-layer correlations (r=0.649-0.683) confirm the three dimensions capture empirically distinguishable quality facets. These findings demonstrate that theory-driven LLM evaluation can achieve measurement-grade reliability and support systematic identification of where current generative models fall short of human literary production, with direct implications for scalable automated evaluation of open-ended text generation.

</details>


### [71] [Retrieve, Integrate, and Synthesize: Spatial-Semantic Grounded Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07106)
*Jin Cui,Xinyue Long,Xunyong Zhang,Yadong Zhang,Chuanchang Su,Jingye Gan,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为RIS（Retrieve, Integrate, and Synthesize）的框架，通过空间-语义对齐的潜在推理机制，增强多模态大语言模型中的细粒度视觉推理能力，解决了现有方法中潜在轨迹不兼容、信息丢失和绕过等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型在视觉-语言推理中常将视觉信息压缩为离散文本思维，导致信息瓶颈；而潜在空间推理方法则存在流形不兼容、轨迹偏离预训练推理结构等问题，限制了细粒度感知能力。

Method: 提出RIS框架：1）构建带有边界框和区域语义描述的逐步对齐推理数据集；2）在连续隐状态中锚定空间与语义证据；3）通过渐进注意力瓶颈强化其因果作用；4）引入短语言过渡标记连接隐状态与词表解码。

Result: 在V*, HRBench4K, HRBench8K, MMVP 和 BLINK 等多个基准上，RIS均优于开源/闭源及潜在推理基线方法；分析显示其学习到多样化、可解释且逐步融合的潜在推理轨迹。

Conclusion: RIS实现了与预训练MLLM计算兼容的潜在推理扩展，为多模态大语言模型实现真实可信的内部视觉推理提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made remarkable progress on vision-language reasoning, yet most methods still compress visual evidence into discrete textual thoughts, creating an information bottleneck for fine-grained perception. Recent latent visual reasoning methods attempt to reason in continuous hidden states, but we find that they suffer from insufficient manifold compatibility: latent trajectories drift away from pretrained reasoning circuits, collapse into instance-agnostic patterns, and are often bypassed during answer generation. To address these issues, we propose RIS (Retrieve, Integrate, and Synthesize), a spatial-semantic grounded framework that develops latent reasoning as a compatible extension of pretrained MLLM computation. We first construct a step-wise grounded reasoning dataset with bounding boxes and region-specific semantic descriptions. Built on this supervision, RIS anchors latent tokens to both spatial and semantic evidence, enforces their causal role through a progressive attention bottleneck, and introduces short language transition tokens to bridge synthesized latent states back to vocabulary-aligned decoding. Experiments on V*, HRBench4K, HRBench8K, MMVP, and BLINK show consistent improvements over closed/open-source and latent reasoning baselines. Further analyses demonstrate that RIS learns diverse, interpretable, and progressively integrated latent trajectories, offering a practical path toward faithful internal visual reasoning in MLLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Beyond LoRA vs. Full Fine-Tuning: Gradient-Guided Optimizer Routing for LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07111)
*Haozhan Tang,Xiuqi Zhu,Xinyin Zhang,Boxun Li,Virginia Smith,Kevin Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoLF的统一微调框架，能够在全量微调（FFT）和低秩适应（LoRA）之间动态切换，提升多种任务下的模型性能，并在资源受限环境下通过MoLF-Efficient实现更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调方法中，全量微调与低秩适应各有优势，但单独使用任一方法存在结构局限性，难以适应不同任务对参数更新的需求。

Method: 提出MoLF框架，在优化器层面动态分配梯度更新至FFT和LoRA两个分支，并引入仅在LoRA专家间路由更新的高效版本MoLF-Efficient以节省内存。

Result: 实验表明，MoLF在各类任务和模型上均达到或接近FFT与LoRA中的较优者（差距≤1.5%），而MoLF-Efficient在事实类任务上比先前自适应LoRA方法最高提升20%，在医学问答和SQL任务上提升达9%。

Conclusion: MoLF及其高效版本能够灵活结合全量微调与低秩适应的优势，克服单一架构的限制，适用于多样化任务并具备良好的实用性与扩展潜力。

Abstract: Recent literature on fine-tuning Large Language Models highlights a fundamental debate. While Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) provides the representational plasticity required for high-entropy knowledge injection, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can match or surpass FFT performance because many tasks only require updates in a low-rank space and benefit from LoRA's additional regularization. Through empirical evaluation across diverse tasks (SQL, Medical QA, and Counterfactual Knowledge) and varying language models (Gemma-3-1B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-3B), we verify both trends and demonstrate that relying solely on either static architecture is structurally limited. To address this challenge, we propose a Mixture of LoRA and Full (MoLF) Fine-Tuning, a unified framework that enables continuous navigation between both training regimes. MoLF dynamically routes updates between FFT and LoRA at the optimizer level to ensure that exact gradient signals are available to both experts throughout training, yielding stable training dynamics. For memory-constrained environments, we also introduce MoLF-Efficient, which freezes base weights and only routes updates among a pair of LoRA experts of potentially varying rank. Our evaluations show that MoLF either improves on or stays within $1.5\%$ of the better of FFT and LoRA across all settings, while MoLF-Efficient outperforms prior adaptive LoRA approaches by up to $20\%$ on Fact and $9\%$ on Med and SQL.

</details>


### [73] [Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07134)
*Donguk Kwon,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于功能区域的网页观察新方法Region4Web，并通过PageDigest框架在WebArena基准上验证了其能有效缩短观察长度并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体多在元素级别进行观察和操作，忽略了网页的功能结构，导致观察冗余且效率低下。本文旨在探索更合理的观察粒度，以显式建模网页的功能组织。

Method: 提出Region4Web框架，通过对AXTree进行层次分解与语义抽象，将网页划分为功能区域；并设计PageDigest推理流程，生成跨步骤持久的页面摘要，供智能体使用。

Result: 在WebArena基准测试中，PageDigest显著减少了观察序列长度，同时提升了多种大语言模型和智能体方法的任务成功率，且不受模型容量影响。

Conclusion: 以功能区域为粒度的观察方式比传统的元素级处理更紧凑、信息更丰富，有助于提升网页智能体的性能。

Abstract: Web agents perceive web pages through an observation space, yet its granularity has remained an underexamined design choice. Existing work treats observation at the same element-level granularity as the action space, leaving the page's functional organization implicit and forcing the agent to infer it from element-level signals at every step. We argue observation should instead operate at the granularity of functional regions, parts of the page that each serve a distinct purpose. We propose Region4Web, a framework that reorganizes the AXTree into functional regions through hierarchical decomposition and semantic abstraction, exposing the page's functional organization as the basis for page state understanding. Moreover, we propose PageDigest, a web-specific inference pipeline that delivers this region-level observation to the actor agent as a compact per-page digest that persists across steps. On the WebArena benchmark, PageDigest substantially reduces observation length while improving overall task success rate across diverse backbone large language models (LLMs) and established agent methods, regardless of backbone capacity. These results show that operating at the granularity of functional regions delivers a more compact and informative basis for the actor agent than element-level processing alone.

</details>


### [74] [Structural Rationale Distillation via Reasoning Space Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07139)
*Jialin Yang,Jiankun Wang,Jiajun Wu,Henry Leung,Jiayu Zhou,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过推理路径压缩进行知识蒸馏的方法（D-RPC），通过构建可重用的高层推理路径库，提升小模型在数学和常识推理任务上的蒸馏效果，实现更一致且高效的监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在类似问题上生成的推理过程不一致，导致对小型学生模型的监督噪声大、难以学习，因此需要一种更稳定、结构化的蒸馏方式。

Method: 提出D-RPC方法，维护一个动态更新的紧凑推理路径库，对每个训练问题检索最相关路径，并引导教师模型据此生成一致性推理过程，结合PAC-Bayes理论分析路径库大小与覆盖范围的权衡。
